C_{{\mathrm{init}}3}>0
is an absolute constant.

S5.3
Proof of Proposition
2

Note that
\boldsymbol{A}_{k}=\boldsymbol{G}_{k}
for
1\leq k\leq p
, and for any
h\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{A}_{p+h}=\sum_{j=1}^{r}\ell_{h}^{I}(\lambda_{j})\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\left\{\ell_{h}^{II,1}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m})\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m-1}+\ell_{h}^{II,2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m})\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m}\right\}.

(S5)

Then
\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{k}=\boldsymbol{G}_{k}-\boldsymbol{G}_{k}^{*}
for
1\leq k\leq p
.
Moreover, for any
h\geq 1
, by (
S5
) and the Taylor expansion,

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{p+h}

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{A}_{p+h}-\boldsymbol{A}_{p+h}^{*}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{r}\Bigg\{\ell_{h}^{I}(\lambda_{j}^{*})+\nabla\ell_{h}^{I}(\lambda_{j}^{*})(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j}^{*})+\frac{1}{2}\nabla^{2}\ell_{h}^{I}(\widetilde{\lambda}_{j})(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j}^{*})^{2}\Bigg\}\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\Bigg\{\ell_{h}^{II,1}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})+(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})^{\top}\nabla\ell_{h}^{II,1}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt+\frac{1}{2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})^{\top}\nabla^{2}\ell_{h}^{II,1}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\eta}}_{m})(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})\Bigg\}\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m-1}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\Bigg\{\ell_{h}^{II,2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})+(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})^{\top}\nabla\ell_{h}^{II,2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt+\frac{1}{2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})^{\top}\nabla^{2}\ell_{h}^{II,2}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\eta}}_{m})(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})\Bigg\}\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m}-\boldsymbol{A}_{p+h}^{*}

\displaystyle:=\boldsymbol{H}_{h}+\boldsymbol{R}_{h},

(S6)

where
\widetilde{\lambda}_{j}
lies between
\lambda_{j}^{*}
and
\lambda_{j}
for
1\leq j\leq r
,
\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\eta}}_{m}
lies between
\boldsymbol{\eta}^{*}_{k}
and
\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}
for
1\leq m\leq s
, the first-order approximation is

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{H}_{h}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{r}\ell_{h}^{I}(\lambda_{j}^{*})(\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}-\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}^{*})+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\sum_{\iota=1}^{2}\ell_{h}^{II,\iota}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})(\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}-\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}^{*})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\sum_{j=1}^{r}(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j}^{*})\nabla\ell_{h}^{I}(\lambda_{j}^{*})\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}^{*}+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\sum_{\iota=1}^{2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})^{\top}\nabla\ell_{h}^{II,\iota}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}^{*},

(S7)

and the remainder is

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{R}_{h}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{r}\nabla\ell_{h}^{I}(\lambda_{j}^{*})(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j}^{*})(\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}-\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}^{*})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\sum_{\iota=1}^{2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})^{\top}\nabla\ell_{h}^{II,\iota}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})(\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}-\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}^{*})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{r}\nabla^{2}\ell_{h}^{I}(\widetilde{\lambda}_{j})(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j}^{*})^{2}\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{m=1}^{s}\sum_{\iota=1}^{2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})^{\top}\nabla^{2}\ell_{h}^{II,\iota}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\eta}}_{m})(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}.

(S8)

Here for notational simplicity, we have suppressed the dependence of
\widetilde{\lambda}_{j}
’s and
\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\eta}}_{m}
’s on
h
.

We first consider
\boldsymbol{R}_{h}
. Denote
\boldsymbol{R}_{h}=\boldsymbol{R}_{1h}+\boldsymbol{R}_{2h}+\boldsymbol{R}_{3h}
, where

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{R}_{1h}=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{r}\nabla\ell_{h}^{I}(\lambda_{j}^{*})(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j}^{*})(\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}-\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}^{*})

\displaystyle+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\sum_{\iota=1}^{2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})^{\top}\nabla\ell_{h}^{II,\iota}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})(\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}-\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}^{*}),

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{R}_{2h}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{r}\nabla^{2}\ell_{h}^{I}(\widetilde{\lambda}_{j})(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j}^{*})^{2}(\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}-\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}^{*})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{m=1}^{s}\sum_{\iota=1}^{2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})^{\top}\nabla^{2}\ell_{h}^{II,\iota}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\eta}}_{m})(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})(\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}-\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}^{*}),

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{R}_{3h}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{r}\nabla^{2}\ell_{h}^{I}(\widetilde{\lambda}_{j})(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j}^{*})^{2}\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}^{*}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{m=1}^{s}\sum_{\iota=1}^{2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})^{\top}\nabla^{2}\ell_{h}^{II,\iota}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\eta}}_{m})(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}^{*}.

(S9)

Note that for any matrix
\boldsymbol{Y}=\sum_{k=1}^{d}a_{k}\boldsymbol{X}_{k}
,

\|\boldsymbol{Y}\|_{{\mathrm{op}}}\leq\|\boldsymbol{Y}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}\leq(\sum_{k=1}^{d}\|\boldsymbol{X}_{k}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}^{2})^{1/2}(\sum_{k=1}^{d}a_{k}^{2})^{1/2}=\|\boldsymbol{X}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}\|\boldsymbol{a}\|_{2}
,
and
\sum_{k=1}^{d}a_{k}^{4}\leq(\sum_{k=1}^{d}a_{k}^{2})^{2}
, where
\boldsymbol{a}=(a_{1},\dots,a_{d})^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, and
\boldsymbol{X}=(\boldsymbol{X}_{1},\dots,\boldsymbol{X}_{d})
.
Then, by Lemma
S2
,

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{R}_{1h}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\ell}\bar{\rho}^{h}\sqrt{\|\boldsymbol{\lambda}-\boldsymbol{\lambda}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}+2\|\boldsymbol{\eta}-\boldsymbol{\eta}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\cdot\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{r}\|\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}-\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}^{*}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}^{2}+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\sum_{\iota=1}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}-\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}^{*}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2}C_{\ell}\bar{\rho}^{h}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}\cdot\|\boldsymbol{G}_{\mathrm{MA}}-\boldsymbol{G}_{\mathrm{MA}}^{*}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}\leq\sqrt{2}C_{\ell}\bar{\rho}^{h}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}\|\boldsymbol{d}\|_{2},

and similarly,

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{R}_{2h}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}C_{\ell}\bar{\rho}^{h}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\|\boldsymbol{G}_{\mathrm{MA}}-\boldsymbol{G}_{\mathrm{MA}}^{*}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}C_{\ell}\bar{\rho}^{h}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{d}\|_{2},

where
\boldsymbol{G}_{\mathrm{MA}}=(\boldsymbol{G}_{p+1},\dots,\boldsymbol{G}_{d})
.
Moreover, by Lemma
S2
again, we can show that

\|\boldsymbol{R}_{3h}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}C_{\ell}\overline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}}\bar{\rho}^{h}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}^{2}.

As a result,

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{R}_{h}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{R}_{1h}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}+\|\boldsymbol{R}_{2h}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}+\|\boldsymbol{R}_{3h}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\ell}\bar{\rho}^{h}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}\left(\sqrt{2}\|\boldsymbol{d}\|_{2}+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}\|\boldsymbol{d}\|_{2}+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\overline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}\right).

(S10)

Now consider
\boldsymbol{H}_{h}
in (
S5.3
).
Notice that for any
h\geq 1
and
1\leq m\leq s
,

\displaystyle\nabla_{\gamma}\ell_{h}^{II,1}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m})=h\gamma_{m}^{h-1}\cos(h\theta_{m})=\frac{1}{\gamma_{m}}\nabla_{\theta}\ell_{h}^{II,2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}),

\displaystyle\nabla_{\gamma}\ell_{h}^{II,2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m})=h\gamma_{m}^{h-1}\sin(h\theta_{m})=-\frac{1}{\gamma_{m}}\nabla_{\theta}\ell_{h}^{II,1}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}).

Thus, the last term on the right side of (
S5.3
) can be simplified to

\displaystyle\sum_{m=1}^{s}\sum_{\iota=1}^{2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})^{\top}\nabla\ell_{h}^{II,\iota}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}^{*}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\sum_{m=1}^{s}\left[(\theta_{m}-\theta_{m}^{*})\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m-1}^{*}-\frac{1}{\gamma_{m}^{*}}(\gamma_{m}-\gamma_{m}^{*})\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m}^{*}\right]\nabla_{\theta}\ell_{h}^{II,1}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\left[(\theta_{m}-\theta_{m}^{*})\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m}^{*}+\frac{1}{\gamma_{m}^{*}}(\gamma_{m}-\gamma_{m}^{*})\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m-1}^{*}\right]\nabla_{\theta}\ell_{h}^{II,2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*}).

(S11)

Let
\boldsymbol{H}=(\boldsymbol{H}_{1},\boldsymbol{H}_{2},\dots)
and
\boldsymbol{R}=(\boldsymbol{R}_{1},\boldsymbol{R}_{2},\dots)
. Then by (
S5.3
) and (
S5.3
) it can be verified that

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\widetilde{H}}:=(\boldsymbol{G}_{1}-\boldsymbol{G}_{1}^{*},\cdots,\boldsymbol{G}_{p}-\boldsymbol{G}_{p}^{*},\boldsymbol{H})

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{D}(\boldsymbol{L}(\boldsymbol{\omega}^{*})\otimes\boldsymbol{I}_{N})^{\top}+\boldsymbol{M}(\boldsymbol{\phi})(\boldsymbol{P}(\boldsymbol{\omega}^{*})\otimes\boldsymbol{I}_{N})^{\top}

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{G}_{\rm{stack}}(\boldsymbol{\phi},\boldsymbol{d})(\boldsymbol{L}_{\rm{stack}}(\boldsymbol{\omega}^{*})\otimes\boldsymbol{I}_{N})^{\top}.

(S12)

Note that

\boldsymbol{\Delta}=\boldsymbol{\widetilde{H}}+(\boldsymbol{0}_{N\times Np},\boldsymbol{R}).

(S13)

Moreover,

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{M}(\boldsymbol{\phi})\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{r}(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j}^{*})^{2}\|\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}^{*}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}^{2}+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\left\|(\theta_{m}-\theta_{m}^{*})\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m-1}^{*}-\frac{\gamma_{m}-\gamma_{m}^{*}}{\gamma_{m}^{*}}\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m}^{*}\right\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\left\|(\theta_{m}-\theta_{m}^{*})\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m}^{*}+\frac{\gamma_{m}-\gamma_{m}^{*}}{\gamma_{m}^{*}}\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m-1}^{*}\right\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{r}(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j}^{*})^{2}\|\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}^{*}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}^{2}+\sum_{m=1}^{s}(\theta_{m}-\theta_{m}^{*})^{2}(\|\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m-1}^{*}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m}^{*}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}^{2})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\frac{(\gamma_{m}-\gamma_{m}^{*})^{2}}{\gamma_{m}^{*2}}(\|\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m-1}^{*}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m}^{*}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}^{2}),

which leads to

\underline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}\leq\|\boldsymbol{M}(\boldsymbol{\phi})\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}\overline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}}}{\min_{1\leq k\leq s}\gamma_{k}^{*}}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}.

(S14)

By the simple inequalities
(|x|+|y|)/2\leq\sqrt{x^{2}+y^{2}}\leq|x|+|y|
, we have

0.5(\|\boldsymbol{d}\|_{2}+\|\boldsymbol{M}(\boldsymbol{\phi})\|_{{\mathrm{F}}})\leq\|\boldsymbol{G}_{\rm{stack}}(\boldsymbol{\phi},\boldsymbol{d})\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}\leq\|\boldsymbol{d}\|_{2}+\|\boldsymbol{M}(\boldsymbol{\phi})\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}
,
and thus in view of (
S14
) we further have

\frac{1}{2}(\|\boldsymbol{d}\|_{2}+\underline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2})\leq\|\boldsymbol{G}_{\rm{stack}}(\boldsymbol{\phi},\boldsymbol{d})\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}\leq\|\boldsymbol{d}\|_{2}+\frac{\sqrt{2}\overline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}}}{\min_{1\leq k\leq s}\gamma_{k}^{*}}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}.

(S15)

Then it follows from (
S15
) that

\frac{\sigma_{\min,L}}{2}(\|\boldsymbol{d}\|_{2}+\underline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2})\leq\|\boldsymbol{\widetilde{H}}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}\leq\sigma_{\max,L}\left(\|\boldsymbol{d}\|_{2}+\frac{\sqrt{2}\overline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}}}{\min_{1\leq k\leq s}\gamma_{k}^{*}}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}\right),

where
\sigma_{\min,L}=\sigma_{\min}(\boldsymbol{L}_{\rm{stack}}(\boldsymbol{\omega}^{*}))
and
\sigma_{\max,L}=\sigma_{\max}(\boldsymbol{L}_{\rm{stack}}(\boldsymbol{\omega}^{*}))
.
Combining this with (
S5.3
), (
S13
), (
S14
), as well as the fact that
\|\boldsymbol{G}_{\mathrm{MA}}-\boldsymbol{G}_{\mathrm{MA}}^{*}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}\leq\|\boldsymbol{d}\|_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{\widetilde{H}}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}+\|\boldsymbol{R}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\{\sigma_{\max,L}+\frac{\sqrt{2}C_{\ell}}{1-\bar{\rho}}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}+\frac{\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}^{2}}{2}\right)\right\}\|\boldsymbol{d}\|_{2}+\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}\sigma_{\max,L}}{\min_{1\leq k\leq s}\gamma_{k}^{*}}+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{C_{\ell}}{1-\bar{\rho}}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}\right)\overline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}

and

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}

\displaystyle\geq\|\boldsymbol{\widetilde{H}}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}-\|\boldsymbol{R}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}

\displaystyle\geq\left\{\frac{\sigma_{\min,L}}{2}-\frac{\sqrt{2}C_{\ell}}{1-\bar{\rho}}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}+\frac{\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}^{2}}{2}\right)\right\}\|\boldsymbol{d}\|_{2}+\left(\frac{\sigma_{\min,L}}{2}-\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{C_{\ell}\overline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}}{(1-\bar{\rho})\underline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}}}\right)\underline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}.

Thus, as long as

\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}\leq c_{\boldsymbol{\omega}}\leq\min\left\{2,\frac{\underline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}}(1-\bar{\rho})\sigma_{\min,L}}{8\sqrt{2}C_{\ell}\overline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}}}\right\},

(S16)

we have

c_{\Delta}\left(\|\boldsymbol{d}\|_{2}+\underline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}\right)\leq\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}\leq C_{\Delta}\left(\|\boldsymbol{d}\|_{2}+\overline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}\right),

(S17)

where

c_{\Delta}=\sigma_{\min,L}/4\quad\text{and}\quad C_{\Delta}=\sigma_{\max,L}\left(1\vee\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\nu_{\mathrm{lower}}^{*}}\right)+\frac{4\sqrt{2}C_{\ell}}{1-\bar{\rho}}.

Finally, by Lemma
S3
, we have

0<(1\wedge c_{\bar{\rho}})/4\leq c_{\Delta}\leq C_{\Delta}\leq(1\vee C_{\bar{\rho}})\left(1\vee\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\nu_{\mathrm{lower}}^{*}}\right)+\frac{4\sqrt{2}C_{\ell}}{1-\bar{\rho}},

i.e.,
c_{\Delta}\asymp 1
and
C_{\Delta}\asymp 1
, and (
S16
) is fulfilled by taking

c_{\boldsymbol{\omega}}=\min\left\{2,\frac{\underline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}}(1-\bar{\rho})(1\wedge c_{\bar{\rho}})}{8\sqrt{2}C_{\ell}\overline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}}}\right\}.

(S18)

The proof of this proposition is complete.

S5.4
Proof of Theorem
2

Note that
\sum_{h=1}^{t-1}\boldsymbol{A}_{h}\boldsymbol{y}_{t-h}=\boldsymbol{A}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{x}}_{t}
, where
\boldsymbol{\widetilde{x}}_{t}=(\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}^{\top},\dots,\boldsymbol{y}_{1}^{\top},0,0,\dots)^{\top}
is the initialized version of
\boldsymbol{x}_{t}
. By the optimality of
\boldsymbol{\widehat{A}}
, we have

\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{A}^{*}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{x}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{x}}_{t}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{A}^{*}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{x}}_{t}\|_{2}^{2}+\lambda_{g}(\|\boldsymbol{g}^{*}\|_{1}-\|\widehat{\boldsymbol{g}}\|_{1}),

Then, since
\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{A}^{*}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{x}}_{t}=\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{t}+\sum_{h=t}^{\infty}\boldsymbol{A}_{h}^{*}\boldsymbol{y}_{t-h}
and
\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{x}}_{t}=\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=t}^{\infty}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}\boldsymbol{y}_{t-k}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{x}}_{t}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{t},\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{x}}_{t}\rangle+\underbrace{\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\sum_{h=t}^{\infty}\boldsymbol{A}_{h}^{*}\boldsymbol{y}_{t-h},\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{x}}_{t}\rangle}_{S_{2}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}})}+\lambda_{g}(\|\boldsymbol{g}^{*}\|_{1}-\|\widehat{\boldsymbol{g}}\|_{1})

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{t},\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\rangle+\lambda_{g}(\|\boldsymbol{g}^{*}\|_{1}-\|\widehat{\boldsymbol{g}}\|_{1})+S_{2}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}})-S_{1}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}),

(S19)

where
S_{1}(\cdot)
and
S_{2}(\cdot)
are defined as in (
S2
).
Moreover, applying the inequality
\|\boldsymbol{a}-\boldsymbol{b}\|_{2}^{2}\geq(3/4)\|\boldsymbol{a}\|_{2}^{2}-3\|\boldsymbol{b}\|_{2}^{2}
with
\boldsymbol{a}=\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}=\sum_{h=1}^{\infty}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}_{h}\boldsymbol{y}_{t-h}
and
\boldsymbol{b}=\sum_{k=t}^{\infty}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}\boldsymbol{y}_{t-k}
, we can lower bound the left-hand side of (
S5.4
) to further obtain that

\displaystyle\frac{3}{4T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\|_{2}^{2}-S_{3}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{t},\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\rangle+\lambda_{g}(\|\boldsymbol{g}^{*}\|_{1}-\|\widehat{\boldsymbol{g}}\|_{1})+S_{2}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}})-S_{1}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}),

(S20)

where
S_{3}(\cdot)
is defined as in (
S2
).
It is worth pointing out that
S_{i}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}})
for
1\leq i\leq 3
capture the initialization effect of
\boldsymbol{y}_{s}=\boldsymbol{0}
for
s\leq 0
on the estimation error, and their upper bounds are given in Lemmas
S6
–
S8
.

Next we assume that the high probability events in Lemmas
S4
–
S8
all hold and focus on the deterministic analysis. For a threshold
\eta>0
to be chosen later, define the thresholded subsets

\displaystyle S_{\mathrm{AR}}(\eta)

\displaystyle=\{(i,j,k)\mid|g_{i,j,k}^{*}|>\eta,i,j\in\{1,\dots,N\},k\in\{1,\dots,p\}\},

\displaystyle S_{\mathrm{MA}}(\eta)

\displaystyle=\{(i,j,k)\mid|g_{i,j,k}^{*}|>\eta,i,j\in\{1,\dots,N\},k\in\{p+1,\dots,d\}\},

and

S(\eta)=S_{\mathrm{AR}}(\eta)\cup S_{\mathrm{MA}}(\eta)=\{(i,j,k)\mid|g_{i,j,k}^{*}|>\eta,i,j\in\{1,\dots,N\},k\in\{1,\dots,d\}\}.

Define
S^{\complement}(\eta)=\{(i,j,k)\mid i,j\in\{1,\dots,N\},k\in\{1,\dots,d\}\}\setminus S(\eta)
as the complementary set of
S(\eta)
. Similarly, the complementary set of
S_{\mathrm{MA}}(\eta)
is
S_{\mathrm{MA}}^{\complement}(\eta)=\{(i,j,k)\mid i,j\in\{1,\dots,N\},k\in\{p+1,\dots,d\}\}\setminus S_{\mathrm{MA}}(\eta)
. Let
|S|
denote the cardinality of a set
S
.
Note that

R_{q}\geq\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\sum_{k=1}^{d}|g_{i,j,k}^{*}|^{q}\geq\sum_{(i,j,k)\in S(\eta)}|g_{i,j,k}^{*}|^{q}\geq\eta^{q}|S(\eta)|,

and

\|\boldsymbol{g}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}^{*}\|_{1}=\sum_{(i,j,k)\in S^{\complement}(\eta)}|g_{i,j,k}^{*}|=\sum_{(i,j,k)\in S^{\complement}(\eta)}|g_{i,j,k}^{*}|^{q}|g_{i,j,k}^{*}|^{1-q}.

Thus, we have

|S(\eta)|\leq R_{q}\eta^{-q}\quad\text{and}\quad\|\boldsymbol{g}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}^{*}\|_{1}\leq R_{q}\eta^{1-q}.

(S21)

Similarly, we can show that

|S_{\mathrm{MA}}(\eta)|\leq R_{q}^{\mathrm{MA}}\eta^{-q}\quad\text{and}\quad\|(\boldsymbol{g}^{*}_{{\mathrm{MA}}})_{S_{\mathrm{MA}}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}\leq R_{q}^{\mathrm{MA}}\eta^{1-q}.

(S22)

By (
S22
), by choosing
\eta
such that

\eta^{2-q}\leq\frac{(r+2s)\overline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}}^{2}}{R_{q}^{\mathrm{MA}}},

(S23)

we have

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{g}_{{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}^{2}\leq 2\|(\boldsymbol{g}_{{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*})_{S_{\mathrm{MA}}(\eta)}\|_{1}^{2}+2\|(\boldsymbol{g}_{{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*})_{S^{\complement}_{\mathrm{MA}}(\eta)}\|_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2|S_{\mathrm{MA}}(\eta)|\|\boldsymbol{g}_{{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}+2(R_{q}^{\mathrm{MA}}\eta^{1-q})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2R_{q}^{\mathrm{MA}}\eta^{-q}\left\{(r+2s)\overline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}}^{2}+R_{q}^{\mathrm{MA}}\eta^{2-q}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq 4R_{q}^{\mathrm{MA}}\eta^{-q}(r+2s)\overline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}}^{2}.

Then, since
r+2s\lesssim 1
and
(\overline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}}/\underline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}})^{2}\lesssim R_{q}/R_{q}^{\mathrm{MA}}
, we further have

\underline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}}^{-2}\|\boldsymbol{g}_{{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}^{2}\lesssim R_{q}\eta^{-q}.

(S24)

Consider the right-hand side of (
S5.4
). By Lemma
S4
, if we choose
\lambda_{g}
such that

\frac{\lambda_{g}}{4}\geq C_{{\mathrm{dev}}}\sqrt{\frac{\kappa_{2}\lambda_{\max}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon})\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}}{T}},

(S25)

then we can show that

\displaystyle\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{t},\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\rangle+\lambda_{g}(\|\boldsymbol{g}^{*}\|_{1}-\|\widehat{\boldsymbol{g}}\|_{1})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\frac{\lambda_{g}}{2}(\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{d}}\|_{1}+\|\boldsymbol{g}_{{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\phi}}\|_{2})+\lambda_{g}(\|\boldsymbol{g}^{*}\|_{1}-\|\boldsymbol{g}_{S(\eta)}^{*}+\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}+\|\boldsymbol{g}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}^{*}+\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{S(\eta)}\|_{1})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\frac{\lambda_{g}}{2}(\|\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{S(\eta)}\|_{1}+\|\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}+\|\boldsymbol{g}_{{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\phi}}\|_{2})+\lambda_{g}(2\|\boldsymbol{g}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}^{*}\|_{1}+\|\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{S(\eta)}\|_{1}-\|\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\frac{\lambda_{g}}{2}\left(4\|\boldsymbol{g}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}^{*}\|_{1}+3\|\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{S(\eta)}\|_{1}-\|\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}+\|\boldsymbol{g}_{{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\phi}}\|_{2}\right).

(S26)

In addition, since
T\gtrsim\kappa_{2}(p\vee 1)^{4}
, it follows from Lemmas
S6
and
S7
that

\displaystyle S_{2}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}})-S_{1}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\lambda_{g}}{4}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{d}}\|_{1}+\|\boldsymbol{g}_{{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\phi}}\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\lambda_{g}}{4}\left(\|\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{S(\eta)}\|_{1}+\|\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}+\|\boldsymbol{g}_{{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\phi}}\|_{2}\right).

(S27)

Combining (
S5.4
), (
S5.4
) and (
S5.4
), we have

\displaystyle 0\leq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{x}}_{t}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{t},\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\rangle+\lambda_{g}(\|\boldsymbol{g}^{*}\|_{1}-\|\widehat{\boldsymbol{g}}\|_{1})+S_{2}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}})-S_{1}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\lambda_{g}}{4}\left(8\|\boldsymbol{g}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}^{*}\|_{1}+7\|\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{S(\eta)}\|_{1}-\|\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}+3\|\boldsymbol{g}_{{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\phi}}\|_{2}\right),

which implies

\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{d}}\|_{1}=\|\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{S(\eta)}\|_{1}+\|\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}\leq 8\|\boldsymbol{g}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}^{*}\|_{1}+8\|\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{S(\eta)}\|_{1}+3\|\boldsymbol{g}_{{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\phi}}\|_{2}.

Then, by the Cauchy-Schwarz inequalty, (
S17
), (
S21
), and (
S24
), we can further show that

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{d}}\|_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3\left(64\|\boldsymbol{g}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}^{*}\|_{1}^{2}+64\|\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{S(\eta)}\|_{1}^{2}+9\|\boldsymbol{g}_{{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\phi}}\|_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 192\|\boldsymbol{g}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}^{*}\|_{1}^{2}+c_{\Delta}^{-2}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}^{2}\left\{192|S(\eta)|+27\underline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}}^{-2}\|\boldsymbol{g}_{{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq 192\|\boldsymbol{g}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}^{*}\|_{1}^{2}+C_{1}c_{\Delta}^{-2}R_{q}\eta^{-q}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}^{2},

(S28)

for an absolute constant
C_{1}>0
.
Similarly, from (
S5.4
) and (
S5.4
), we can deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{t},\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\rangle+\lambda_{g}(\|\boldsymbol{g}^{*}\|_{1}-\|\widehat{\boldsymbol{g}}\|_{1})+S_{2}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}})-S_{1}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\frac{\lambda_{g}}{4}\left(8\|\boldsymbol{g}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}^{*}\|_{1}+8\|\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{S(\eta)}\|_{1}+3\|\boldsymbol{g}_{{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\phi}}\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\frac{\lambda_{g}}{2}\left\{4\|\boldsymbol{g}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}^{*}\|_{1}+C_{2}c_{\Delta}^{-1}R_{q}^{1/2}\eta^{-q/2}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}\right\},

(S29)

for an absolute constant
C_{2}>0
.

By Lemmas
S5
and
S8
, we can show that

\frac{3}{4T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\|_{2}^{2}-S_{3}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}})\geq\frac{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}\kappa_{1}}{2}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}^{2}-\frac{\kappa_{2}}{T}\left\{C_{{\mathrm{init}}3}(p\vee 1)+\frac{3}{4}C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}\frac{\kappa_{2}}{\kappa_{1}}\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}\right\}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{d}}\|_{1}^{2}.

which, in conjunction with (
S5.4
), leads to

\frac{3}{4T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\|_{2}^{2}-S_{3}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}})\geq\frac{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}\kappa_{1}}{4}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}^{2}-\frac{C_{3}\kappa_{2}^{2}(p\vee 1)\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}}{\kappa_{1}T}\|\boldsymbol{g}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}^{*}\|_{1}^{2},

(S30)

where
C_{3}>0
is an absolute constant, if we further have

T\gtrsim R_{q}\eta^{-q}(\kappa_{2}/\kappa_{1})^{2}(p\vee 1)\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}.

(S31)

Combining (
S20
), (
S5.4
), and (
S30
), we have

\frac{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}\kappa_{1}}{4}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}^{2}-\frac{C_{3}\kappa_{2}^{2}(p\vee 1)\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}}{\kappa_{1}T}\|\boldsymbol{g}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}^{*}\|_{1}^{2}\leq\frac{\lambda_{g}}{2}\left\{4\|\boldsymbol{g}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}^{*}\|_{1}+C_{2}c_{\Delta}^{-1}R_{q}^{1/2}\eta^{-q/2}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}\right\}.

Consider the following two cases.

Case (i):
First suppose that

\frac{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}\kappa_{1}}{8}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}^{2}\geq\frac{C_{3}\kappa_{2}^{2}(p\vee 1)\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}}{\kappa_{1}T}\|\boldsymbol{g}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}^{*}\|_{1}^{2}
.
Then

\frac{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}\kappa_{1}}{8}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}^{2}\leq\frac{\lambda_{g}}{2}\left\{4\|\boldsymbol{g}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}^{*}\|_{1}+C_{2}c_{\Delta}^{-1}R_{q}^{1/2}\eta^{-q/2}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}\right\},

which involves a quadratic form in
\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}
. By computing the zeros of this quadratic form, we can show that

\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}^{2}\leq\frac{32C_{2}^{2}}{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}^{2}c_{\Delta}^{2}}\cdot\frac{\lambda_{g}^{2}R_{q}\eta^{-q}}{\kappa_{1}^{2}}+\frac{32}{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}}\cdot\frac{\lambda_{g}\|\boldsymbol{g}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}^{*}\|_{1}}{\kappa_{1}}.

Case (ii):
Otherwise, we must have
\frac{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}\kappa_{1}}{8}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}^{2}\leq\frac{C_{3}\kappa_{2}^{2}(p\vee 1)\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}}{\kappa_{1}T}\|\boldsymbol{g}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}^{*}\|_{1}^{2}
.

Combining the two cases above, we can apply (
S21
) and (
S31
) to show that

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{32C_{2}^{2}}{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}^{2}c_{\Delta}^{2}}\cdot\frac{\lambda_{g}^{2}R_{q}\eta^{-q}}{\kappa_{1}^{2}}+\frac{32}{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}}\cdot\frac{\lambda_{g}\|\boldsymbol{g}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}^{*}\|_{1}}{\kappa_{1}}+\frac{8C_{3}}{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}}\cdot\frac{\kappa_{2}^{2}(p\vee 1)\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}}{\kappa_{1}^{2}T}\|\boldsymbol{g}_{S^{\complement}(\eta)}^{*}\|_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{32C_{2}^{2}}{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}^{2}c_{\Delta}^{2}}\cdot\frac{\lambda_{g}^{2}R_{q}\eta^{-q}}{\kappa_{1}^{2}}+\frac{32}{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}}\cdot\frac{\lambda_{g}R_{q}\eta^{1-q}}{\kappa_{1}}+\frac{8C_{3}}{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}}\cdot(R_{q}\eta^{-q})^{-1}(R_{q}\eta^{1-q})^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\frac{\lambda_{g}}{\kappa_{1}}\right)^{2-q}R_{q}=\eta^{2-q}R_{q},

if we choose

\eta=\frac{\lambda_{g}}{\kappa_{1}}.

Thus, taking
\lambda_{g}
as its lower bound in (
S25
), i.e.,

\lambda_{g}\asymp\sqrt{\kappa_{2}\lambda_{\max}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon})\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}/T}
, we have

\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\|_{{\mathrm{F}}}^{2}\lesssim\left[\frac{\kappa_{2}\lambda_{\max}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon})\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}}{\kappa_{1}^{2}T}\right]^{1-q/2}R_{q},

and subsequently,

\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\Delta}}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{x}}_{t}\|_{2}^{2}\lesssim\lambda_{g}\eta^{1-q}R_{q}=\left[\frac{\kappa_{2}\lambda_{\max}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon})\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}}{\kappa_{1}^{2}T}\right]^{1-q/2}\frac{R_{q}}{\kappa_{1}^{1-q}},

where the latter follows from (
S5.4
) and (
S5.4
).
On the one hand, with the above choice of
\eta
, condition (
S31
) can be guaranteed if

R_{q}\lesssim\frac{\lambda_{\max}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon})}{\kappa_{2}(p\vee 1)}\cdot\left[\frac{\kappa_{1}^{2}T}{\kappa_{2}\lambda_{\max}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon})\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}}\right]^{1-q/2}.

(S32)

Under condition (
S32
), since
r+2s\lesssim 1
, we can show that a sufficient condition for (
S23
) is

\frac{\lambda_{\max}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon})}{\kappa_{2}(p\vee 1)}\lesssim\overline{\alpha}_{\mathrm{MA}}^{2}R_{q}/R_{q}^{\mathrm{MA}}.

(S33)

Finally, combining the tail probabilities in Lemmas
S4
–
S8
and the required conditions including (
S32
) and (
S33
), we accomplish the proof of this theorem.

S6
Proofs of Proposition
3
and Theorem
3

S6.1
Notations

For
1\leq i\leq N
, denote
\boldsymbol{\delta}_{i}=\boldsymbol{a}_{i}-\boldsymbol{a}_{i}^{*}=(\boldsymbol{\delta}_{i,1}^{\top},\boldsymbol{\delta}_{i,2}^{\top},\dots)^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{\infty}
and
\boldsymbol{d}_{i}=\boldsymbol{g}_{i}-\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*}
, where
\boldsymbol{\delta}_{i,h}=\boldsymbol{a}_{i,h}-\boldsymbol{a}_{i,h}^{*}=\sum_{k=1}^{d}\ell_{h,k}(\boldsymbol{\omega})\boldsymbol{g}_{i,k}-\sum_{k=1}^{d}\ell_{h,k}(\boldsymbol{\omega}^{*})\boldsymbol{g}_{i,k}^{*}
for
h\geq 1
. Given
\boldsymbol{\omega}^{*}
and
\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*}
, define

\boldsymbol{g}_{i,\rm{stack}}(\boldsymbol{\phi},\boldsymbol{d}_{i})=(\boldsymbol{d}_{i}^{\top},(\boldsymbol{m}_{i}(\boldsymbol{\phi}))^{\top})^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{N(d+r+2s)},

where
\boldsymbol{m}_{i}(\boldsymbol{\phi})\in\mathbb{R}^{N(r+2s)}
is the following linear mapping of
\boldsymbol{\phi}
,

\boldsymbol{m}_{i}(\boldsymbol{\phi})=\left(\begin{matrix}(\lambda_{1}-\lambda_{1}^{*})\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+1}^{*}\\
\vdots\\
(\lambda_{r}-\lambda_{r}^{*})\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r}^{*}\\
(\theta_{1}-\theta_{1}^{*})\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+1}^{*}-\frac{\gamma_{1}-\gamma_{1}^{*}}{\gamma_{1}^{*}}\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2}^{*}\\
(\theta_{1}-\theta_{1}^{*})\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2}^{*}+\frac{\gamma_{1}-\gamma_{1}^{*}}{\gamma_{1}^{*}}\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+1}^{*}\\
\vdots\\
(\theta_{s}-\theta_{s}^{*})\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2s-1}^{*}-\frac{\gamma_{s}-\gamma_{s}^{*}}{\gamma_{s}^{*}}\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2s}^{*},\\
(\theta_{s}-\theta_{s}^{*})\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2s}^{*}+\frac{\gamma_{s}-\gamma_{s}^{*}}{\gamma_{s}^{*}}\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2s-1}^{*}\end{matrix}\right).

Note that
\boldsymbol{g}_{i,\rm{stack}}(\boldsymbol{\phi},\boldsymbol{d}_{i})
and
\boldsymbol{m}_{i}(\boldsymbol{\phi})
correspond to the
i
th row of
\boldsymbol{G}_{\rm{stack}}(\boldsymbol{\phi},\boldsymbol{d}_{i})
and
\boldsymbol{M}(\boldsymbol{\phi})
, respectively; see Section
S5.1
. In addition, for
1\leq i\leq N
, let
\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}=\boldsymbol{\widehat{a}}_{i}-\boldsymbol{a}_{i}^{*}
, where
\boldsymbol{\widehat{a}}_{i}=(\boldsymbol{\widehat{a}}_{i,1}^{\top},\boldsymbol{\widehat{a}}_{i,2}^{\top},\dots)^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{\infty}
,

\boldsymbol{\widehat{d}}_{i}=\boldsymbol{\widehat{g}}_{i}-\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*}
, and
\boldsymbol{\widehat{\phi}}_{i}=\boldsymbol{\widehat{\omega}}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\omega}^{*}
.

As will be shown in the proof of Theorem
3
, the following terms quantify the effect of initializing
\boldsymbol{y}_{s}=\boldsymbol{0}
for
s\leq 0
:

\displaystyle\begin{split}&S_{1}(\boldsymbol{\delta}_{i})=\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\varepsilon_{i,t},\sum_{h=t}^{\infty}\boldsymbol{\delta}_{i,h}^{\top}\boldsymbol{y}_{t-h}\rangle\\
&S_{2}(\boldsymbol{\delta}_{i})=\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\langle\sum_{h=t}^{\infty}\boldsymbol{a}_{i,h}^{*\top}\boldsymbol{y}_{t-h},\sum_{k=1}^{t-1}\boldsymbol{\delta}_{i,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{y}_{t-k}\rangle\\
&S_{3}(\boldsymbol{\delta}_{i})=\frac{3}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\sum_{k=t}^{\infty}\boldsymbol{\delta}_{i,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{y}_{t-k}\Big)^{2}.\end{split}

(S1)

Here we use the notations
S_{i}(\cdot)
’s for convenience, while their definitions in this section are different from those in (
S2
).

S6.2
Preliminary results

The proofs of Proposition
3
and Theorem
3
can be regarded as special cases of those of Proposition
2
and Theorem
2
with a univariate response variable.

In Proposition
3
, the goal is to establish the local linearity of
\boldsymbol{\delta}_{i}(\boldsymbol{\phi},\boldsymbol{d})
with respect to
\boldsymbol{\phi}
and
\boldsymbol{d}_{i}
. That is, within a local neighborhood of
\boldsymbol{\omega}^{*}
, we aim to show that

\boldsymbol{\delta}_{i}(\boldsymbol{\phi},\boldsymbol{d}_{i})=\boldsymbol{a}_{i}(\boldsymbol{\omega},\boldsymbol{g}_{i})-\boldsymbol{a}_{i}^{*}\approx(\boldsymbol{L}_{\rm{stack}}(\boldsymbol{\omega}^{*})\otimes\boldsymbol{I}_{N})\boldsymbol{g}_{i,\rm{stack}}(\boldsymbol{\phi},\boldsymbol{d}_{i}).

(S2)

Note that (
S2
) corresponds to the
i
th row of (
S4
).

The proof of Theorem
3
directly relies on Lemmas
S9
–
S13
below. Their proofs are straightforward univariate versions of those of Lemmas
S4
–
S8
, and hence are omitted.

Lemma S9

(Deviation bound)
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
3
, if
\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\omega}}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\omega}^{*}\|_{2}\leq c_{i,\boldsymbol{\omega}}
,
\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\|\boldsymbol{\Psi}_{j}^{*}\|_{{\mathrm{op}}}^{2}<\infty
, and
T\gtrsim\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}
, then with probability at least
1-Ce^{-c\log N}
,

\frac{1}{T}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\varepsilon_{i,t},\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\rangle\right|\leq C_{{\mathrm{dev}}}\sqrt{\frac{\kappa_{2}\lambda_{\max}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon})\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}}{T}}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{d}}_{i}\|_{1}+\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\phi}}_{i}\|_{2}\right),

where
C_{{\mathrm{dev}}}>0
is an absolute constant.

Lemma S10

(Restricted strong convexity)
.

Under Assumptions
1
–
3
, if
\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\omega}}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\omega}^{*}\|_{2}\leq c_{i,\boldsymbol{\omega}}
and
T\gtrsim(\kappa_{2}/\kappa_{1})^{2}\log\{(\kappa_{2}/\kappa_{1})(\overline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}/\underline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}})N(p\vee 1)\}
, then with probability at least
1-Ce^{-c\kappa_{1}^{2}T/\kappa_{2}^{2}}
,

\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{x}_{t})^{2}\geq C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}\left[\kappa_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}-\frac{\kappa_{2}^{2}\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}}{\kappa_{1}T}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{d}}_{i}\|_{1}^{2}\right],

where
C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}>0
is an absolute constant.

Lemma S11

(Effect of initial values I)
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
3
, if
\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\omega}}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\omega}^{*}\|_{2}\leq c_{i,\boldsymbol{\omega}}
,
\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\|\boldsymbol{\Psi}_{j}^{*}\|_{{\mathrm{op}}}^{2}<\infty
, and
T\gtrsim\log N
, then with probability at least
1-C(p\vee 1)e^{-c\log N}
,

|S_{1}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i})|\leq\frac{C_{{\mathrm{init}}1}\sqrt{\kappa_{2}\lambda_{\max}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon})(p\vee 1)\log N}}{T}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{d}}_{i}\|_{1}+\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\phi}}_{i}\|_{2}\right),

where
C_{{\mathrm{init}}1}>0
is an absolute constant.

Lemma S12

(Effect of initial values II)
.

Under Assumptions
1
–
3
, if
\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\omega}}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\omega}^{*}\|_{2}\leq c_{i,\boldsymbol{\omega}}
and
T\gtrsim\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}
, then
with probability at least
1-C(p\vee 1)e^{-c\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}}
,

|S_{2}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i})|\leq\frac{C_{{\mathrm{init}}2}\kappa_{2}(p\vee 1)^{2}}{T}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{d}}_{i}\|_{1}+\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\phi}}_{i}\|_{2}\right),

where
C_{{\mathrm{init}}2}>0
is an absolute constant.

Lemma S13

(Effect of initial values III)
.

Under Assumptions
1
–
3
, if
\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\omega}}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\omega}^{*}\|_{2}\leq c_{i,\boldsymbol{\omega}}
and
\log N\gtrsim(\kappa_{2}/\kappa_{1})^{2}
, then with probability at least
1-Ce^{-c\kappa_{1}^{2}(p\vee 1)\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}/\kappa_{2}^{2}}
,

|S_{3}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i})|\leq\frac{C_{{\mathrm{init}}3}\kappa_{2}(p\vee 1)}{T}\left[\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}+\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{d}}_{i}\|_{1}^{2}\right],

where
C_{{\mathrm{init}}3}>0
is an absolute constant.

S6.3
Proof of Proposition
3

Note that
\boldsymbol{a}_{i,k}=\boldsymbol{g}_{i,k}
for
1\leq k\leq p
, and

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{a}_{i,p+h}=\sum_{j=1}^{r}\ell_{h}^{I}(\lambda_{j})\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+j}+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\left\{\ell_{h}^{II,1}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m})\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2m-1}+\ell_{h}^{II,2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m})\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2m}\right\},\quad\forall h\geq 1.

Then
\boldsymbol{\delta}_{i,k}=\boldsymbol{g}_{i,k}-\boldsymbol{g}_{i,k}^{*}
for
1\leq k\leq p
, and by the Taylor expansion, for any
h\geq 1
, we have

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\delta}_{i,p+h}

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{a}_{i,p+h}-\boldsymbol{a}_{i,p+h}^{*}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{r}\Bigg\{\ell_{h}^{I}(\lambda_{j}^{*})+\nabla\ell_{h}^{I}(\lambda_{j}^{*})(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j}^{*})+\frac{1}{2}\nabla^{2}\ell_{h}^{I}(\widetilde{\lambda}_{j})(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j}^{*})^{2}\Bigg\}\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+j}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\Bigg\{\ell_{h}^{II,1}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})+(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})^{\top}\nabla\ell_{h}^{II,1}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt+\frac{1}{2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})^{\top}\nabla^{2}\ell_{h}^{II,1}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\eta}}_{j})(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})\Bigg\}\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2m-1}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\Bigg\{\ell_{h}^{II,2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})+(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})^{\top}\nabla\ell_{h}^{II,2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt+\frac{1}{2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})^{\top}\nabla^{2}\ell_{h}^{II,2}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\eta}}_{j})(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})\Bigg\}\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2m}-\boldsymbol{a}_{i,p+h}^{*}

\displaystyle:=\boldsymbol{h}_{i,h}+\boldsymbol{r}_{i,h},

(S3)

where
\widetilde{\lambda}_{j}
lies between
\lambda_{j}^{*}
and
\lambda_{j}
for
1\leq j\leq r
,
\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\eta}}_{j}
lies between
\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*}
and
\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}
for
1\leq m\leq s
, the first-order approximation is

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{h}_{i,h}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{r}\ell_{h}^{I}(\lambda_{j}^{*})(\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+j}-\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+j}^{*})+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\sum_{\iota=1}^{2}\ell_{h}^{II,\iota}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})(\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}-\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}^{*})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\sum_{j=1}^{r}(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j}^{*})\nabla\ell_{h}^{I}(\lambda_{j}^{*})\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+j}^{*}+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\sum_{\iota=1}^{2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})^{\top}\nabla\ell_{h}^{II,\iota}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}^{*},

(S4)

and the remainder is

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{r}_{i,h}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{r}\nabla\ell_{h}^{I}(\lambda_{j}^{*})(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j}^{*})(\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+j}-\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+j}^{*})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\sum_{\iota=1}^{2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})^{\top}\nabla\ell_{h}^{II,\iota}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})(\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}-\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}^{*})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{r}\nabla^{2}\ell_{h}^{I}(\widetilde{\lambda}_{j})(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j}^{*})^{2}\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+j}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{m=1}^{s}\sum_{\iota=1}^{2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})^{\top}\nabla^{2}\ell_{h}^{II,\iota}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\eta}}_{j})(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}.

(S5)

Here for notational simplicity, we have suppressed the dependence of
\widetilde{\lambda}_{j}
’s and
\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\eta}}_{j}
’s on
i,h
.

We first consider
\boldsymbol{r}_{i,h}
. Denote
\boldsymbol{r}_{i,h}=\boldsymbol{r}_{i,1h}+\boldsymbol{r}_{i,2h}+\boldsymbol{r}_{i,3h}
, where

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{r}_{i,1h}=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{r}\nabla\ell_{h}^{I}(\lambda_{j}^{*})(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j}^{*})(\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+j}-\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+j}^{*})

\displaystyle+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\sum_{\iota=1}^{2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})^{\top}\nabla\ell_{h}^{II,\iota}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})(\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}-\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}^{*}),

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{r}_{i,2h}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{r}\nabla^{2}\ell_{h}^{I}(\widetilde{\lambda}_{j})(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j}^{*})^{2}(\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+j}-\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+j}^{*})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{m=1}^{s}\sum_{\iota=1}^{2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})^{\top}\nabla^{2}\ell_{h}^{II,\iota}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\eta}}_{j})(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})(\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}-\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}^{*}),

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{r}_{i,3h}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{r}\nabla^{2}\ell_{h}^{I}(\widetilde{\lambda}_{j})(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j}^{*})^{2}\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+j}^{*}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{m=1}^{s}\sum_{\iota=1}^{2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})^{\top}\nabla^{2}\ell_{h}^{II,\iota}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\eta}}_{j})(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}^{*}.

(S6)

Similar to the proof of Proposition
2
, by Lemma
S2
, we can show that

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{r}_{i,1h}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\ell}\bar{\rho}^{h}\sqrt{\|\boldsymbol{\lambda}-\boldsymbol{\lambda}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}+2\|\boldsymbol{\eta}-\boldsymbol{\eta}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\cdot\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{r}\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+j}-\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+j}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\sum_{\iota=1}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}-\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2}C_{\ell}\bar{\rho}^{h}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}\cdot\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}-\boldsymbol{g}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{2}C_{\ell}\bar{\rho}^{h}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}\|\boldsymbol{d}_{i}\|_{2},

and similarly,

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{r}_{i,2h}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}C_{\ell}\bar{\rho}^{h}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}-\boldsymbol{g}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{2}\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}C_{\ell}\bar{\rho}^{h}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{d}_{i}\|_{2}.

Moreover, by Lemma
S2
again, we can show that

\|\boldsymbol{r}_{i,3h}\|_{2}\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}C_{\ell}\overline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}\bar{\rho}^{h}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}^{2}.

As a result,

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{r}_{i,h}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{r}_{i,1h}\|_{2}+\|\boldsymbol{r}_{i,2h}\|_{2}+\|\boldsymbol{r}_{i,3h}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\ell}\bar{\rho}^{h}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}\left(\sqrt{2}\|\boldsymbol{d}_{i}\|_{2}+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}\|\boldsymbol{d}_{i}\|_{2}+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\overline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}\right).

(S7)

Now consider
\boldsymbol{h}_{i,h}
in (
S6.3
).
Notice that for any
h\geq 1
and
1\leq j\leq s
,

\displaystyle\nabla_{\gamma}\ell_{h}^{II,1}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m})=h\gamma_{m}^{h-1}\cos(h\theta_{m})=\frac{1}{\gamma_{m}}\nabla_{\theta}\ell_{h}^{II,2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}),

\displaystyle\nabla_{\gamma}\ell_{h}^{II,2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m})=h\gamma_{m}^{h-1}\sin(h\theta_{m})=-\frac{1}{\gamma_{m}}\nabla_{\theta}\ell_{h}^{II,1}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}).

Thus, the last term on the right side of (
S6.3
) can be simplified to

\displaystyle\sum_{m=1}^{s}\sum_{\iota=1}^{2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})^{\top}\nabla\ell_{h}^{II,\iota}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2(m-1)+\iota}^{*}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\sum_{m=1}^{s}\left[(\theta_{m}-\theta_{m}^{*})\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2m-1}^{*}-\frac{1}{\gamma_{m}^{*}}(\gamma_{m}-\gamma_{m}^{*})\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2m}^{*}\right]\nabla_{\theta}\ell_{h}^{II,1}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\left[(\theta_{m}-\theta_{m}^{*})\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2m}^{*}+\frac{1}{\gamma_{m}^{*}}(\gamma_{m}-\gamma_{m}^{*})\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2m-1}^{*}\right]\nabla_{\theta}\ell_{h}^{II,2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}^{*}).

(S8)

Let
\boldsymbol{h}_{i}=(\boldsymbol{h}_{i,1}^{\top},\boldsymbol{h}_{i,2}^{\top},\dots)^{\top}
and
\boldsymbol{r}_{i}=(\boldsymbol{r}_{i,1}^{\top},\boldsymbol{r}_{i,2}^{\top},\dots)^{\top}
. Then by (
S6.3
) and (
S6.3
) it can be verified that

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\widetilde{h}}_{i}:=((\boldsymbol{g}_{i,1}-\boldsymbol{g}_{i,1}^{*})^{\top},\cdots,(\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p}-\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p}^{*})^{\top},\boldsymbol{h}_{i}^{\top})^{\top}

\displaystyle=(\boldsymbol{L}(\boldsymbol{\omega}^{*})\otimes\boldsymbol{I}_{N})\boldsymbol{d}_{i}+(\boldsymbol{P}(\boldsymbol{\omega}^{*})\otimes\boldsymbol{I}_{N})\boldsymbol{m}_{i}(\boldsymbol{\phi})

\displaystyle=(\boldsymbol{L}_{\rm{stacj}}(\boldsymbol{\omega}^{*})\otimes\boldsymbol{I}_{N})\boldsymbol{g}_{i,\rm{stacj}}(\boldsymbol{\phi},\boldsymbol{d}_{i}).

(S9)

Note that

\boldsymbol{\delta}_{i}=\boldsymbol{\widetilde{h}}_{i}+\left(\begin{matrix}\boldsymbol{0}_{Np}\\
\boldsymbol{r}_{i}\end{matrix}\right)

(S10)

Moreover,

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{m}_{i}(\boldsymbol{\phi})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{r}(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j}^{*})^{2}\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+j}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\left\|(\theta_{m}-\theta_{m}^{*})\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2m-1}^{*}-\frac{\gamma_{m}-\gamma_{m}^{*}}{\gamma_{m}^{*}}\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2m}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\left\|(\theta_{m}-\theta_{m}^{*})\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2m}^{*}+\frac{\gamma_{m}-\gamma_{m}^{*}}{\gamma_{m}^{*}}\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2m-1}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{r}(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{j}^{*})^{2}\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+j}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{m=1}^{s}(\theta_{m}-\theta_{m}^{*})^{2}(\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2m-1}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2m}^{*}\|_{2}^{2})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\frac{(\gamma_{m}-\gamma_{m}^{*})^{2}}{\gamma_{m}^{*2}}(\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2m-1}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2m}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}),

which leads to

\underline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}\leq\|\boldsymbol{m}_{i}(\boldsymbol{\phi})\|_{2}\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}\overline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}}{\min_{1\leq j\leq s}\gamma_{m}^{*}}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}.

(S11)

By the simple inequalities
(|x|+|y|)/2\leq\sqrt{x^{2}+y^{2}}\leq|x|+|y|
, we have

0.5(\|\boldsymbol{d}_{i}\|_{2}+\|\boldsymbol{m}_{i}(\boldsymbol{\phi})\|_{2})\leq\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,\rm{stacj}}(\boldsymbol{\phi},\boldsymbol{d}_{i})\|_{2}\leq\|\boldsymbol{d}_{i}\|_{2}+\|\boldsymbol{m}_{i}(\boldsymbol{\phi})\|_{2}
,
and thus in view of (
S11
) we further have

\frac{1}{2}(\|\boldsymbol{d}_{i}\|_{2}+\underline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2})\leq\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,\rm{stacj}}(\boldsymbol{\phi},\boldsymbol{d}_{i})\|_{2}\leq\|\boldsymbol{d}_{i}\|_{2}+\frac{\sqrt{2}\overline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}}{\min_{1\leq j\leq s}\gamma_{m}^{*}}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}.

(S12)

Then it follows from (
S12
) that

\frac{\sigma_{\min,L}}{2}(\|\boldsymbol{d}_{i}\|_{2}+\underline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2})\leq\|\boldsymbol{\widetilde{h}}\|_{2}\leq\sigma_{\max,L}\left(\|\boldsymbol{d}_{i}\|_{2}+\frac{\sqrt{2}\overline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}}{\min_{1\leq j\leq s}\gamma_{m}^{*}}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}\right).

Combining this with (
S6.3
), (
S10
), (
S11
), as well as the fact that
\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}-\boldsymbol{g}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{2}\leq\|\boldsymbol{d}_{i}\|_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{\delta}_{i}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{\widetilde{h}}_{i}\|_{2}+\|\boldsymbol{r}_{i}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\{\sigma_{\max,L}+\frac{\sqrt{2}C_{\ell}}{1-\bar{\rho}}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}+\frac{\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}^{2}}{2}\right)\right\}\|\boldsymbol{d}_{i}\|_{2}+\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}\overline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}\sigma_{\max,L}}{\min_{1\leq j\leq s}\gamma_{m}^{*}}+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{C_{\ell}\overline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}}{1-\bar{\rho}}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}\right)\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}

and

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{\delta}_{i}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\geq\|\boldsymbol{\widetilde{h}}_{i}\|_{2}-\|\boldsymbol{r}_{i}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\geq\left\{\frac{\sigma_{\min,L}}{2}-\frac{\sqrt{2}C_{\ell}}{1-\bar{\rho}}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}+\frac{\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}^{2}}{2}\right)\right\}\|\boldsymbol{d}_{i}\|_{2}+\left(\frac{\underline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}\sigma_{\min,L}}{2}-\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{C_{\ell}\overline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}}{1-\bar{\rho}}\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}\right)\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}.

Thus, as long as

\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}\leq c_{i,\boldsymbol{\omega}}\leq\min\left\{2,\frac{\underline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}(1-\bar{\rho})\sigma_{\min,L}}{8\sqrt{2}C_{\ell}\overline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}}\right\},

(S13)

we have

c_{\Delta}\left(\|\boldsymbol{d}_{i}\|_{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}\right)\leq\|\boldsymbol{\delta}_{i}\|_{2}\leq C_{\Delta}\left(\|\boldsymbol{d}_{i}\|_{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\phi}\|_{2}\right),

(S14)

where
c_{\Delta}
and
C_{\Delta}
are absolute constants defined as in the proof of Proposition
2
. By Lemma
S3
, (
S13
) is fulfilled by taking

c_{i,\boldsymbol{\omega}}=\min\left\{2,\frac{\underline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}(1-\bar{\rho})(1\wedge c_{\bar{\rho}})}{8\sqrt{2}C_{\ell}\overline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}}\right\}.

(S15)

The proof of this proposition is complete.

S6.4
Proof of Theorem
3

The proof of this theorem closely mirrors that of Theorem
2
.
Note that
\sum_{h=1}^{t-1}\boldsymbol{a}_{i,h}^{\top}\boldsymbol{y}_{t-h}=\boldsymbol{a}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{x}}_{t}
, where
\boldsymbol{\widetilde{x}}_{t}=(\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}^{\top},\dots,\boldsymbol{y}_{1}^{\top},0,0,\dots)^{\top}
is the initialized version of
\boldsymbol{x}_{t}
. By the optimality of
\boldsymbol{\widehat{a}}_{i}
, we have

\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(y_{i,t}-\boldsymbol{a}_{i}^{*\top}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{x}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{x}}_{t})^{2}\leq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(y_{i,t}-\boldsymbol{a}_{i}^{*\top}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{x}}_{t})^{2}+\lambda_{g}(\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*}\|_{1}-\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{g}}_{i}\|_{1}),

Then, since
y_{i,t}-\boldsymbol{a}_{i}^{*\top}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{x}}_{t}=\varepsilon_{i,t}+\sum_{h=t}^{\infty}\boldsymbol{a}_{i,h}^{*\top}\boldsymbol{y}_{t-h}
and
\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{x}}_{t}=\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=t}^{\infty}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{y}_{t-k}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{x}}_{t})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\varepsilon_{i,t},\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{x}}_{t}\rangle+\underbrace{\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\sum_{h=t}^{\infty}\boldsymbol{a}_{i,h}^{*\top}\boldsymbol{y}_{t-h},\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{x}}_{t}\rangle}_{S_{2}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i})}+\lambda_{g}(\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*}\|_{1}-\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{g}}_{i}\|_{1})

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\varepsilon_{i,t},\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\rangle+\lambda_{g}(\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*}\|_{1}-\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{g}}_{i}\|_{1})+S_{2}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i})-S_{1}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}),

(S16)

where
S_{1}(\cdot)
and
S_{2}(\cdot)
are defined as in (
S1
).
Moreover, similar to (
S20
), we can lower bound the left-hand side of (
S6.4
) to further obtain that

\displaystyle\frac{3}{4T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{x}_{t})^{2}-S_{3}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\varepsilon_{i,t},\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\rangle+\lambda_{g}(\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*}\|_{1}-\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{g}}_{i}\|_{1})+S_{2}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i})-S_{1}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}),

(S17)

where
S_{3}(\cdot)
is defined as in (
S1
).

Next we assume that the high probability events in Lemmas
S9
–
S13
all hold and focus on the deterministic analysis. For a threshold
\eta>0
to be chosen later, define the thresholded subsets

\displaystyle S_{i,{\mathrm{AR}}}(\eta)

\displaystyle=\{(j,k)\mid|g_{i,j,k}^{*}|>\eta,j\in\{1,\dots,N\},k\in\{1,\dots,p\}\},

\displaystyle S_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}(\eta)

\displaystyle=\{(j,k)\mid|g_{i,j,k}^{*}|>\eta,j\in\{1,\dots,N\},k\in\{p+1,\dots,d\}\},

and

S_{i}(\eta)=S_{i,{\mathrm{AR}}}(\eta)\cup S_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}(\eta)=\{(j,k)\mid|g_{i,j,k}^{*}|>\eta,j\in\{1,\dots,N\},k\in\{1,\dots,d\}\}.

Define
S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)=\{(j,k)\mid j\in\{1,\dots,N\},k\in\{1,\dots,d\}\}\setminus S_{i}(\eta)
as the complementary set of
S_{i}(\eta)
. Similarly, the complementary set of
S_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}(\eta)
is
S_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{\complement}(\eta)=\{(j,k)\mid j\in\{1,\dots,N\},k\in\{p+1,\dots,d\}\}\setminus S_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}(\eta)
.

Note that

R_{i,q}\geq\sum_{j=1}^{N}\sum_{k=1}^{d}|g_{i,j,k}^{*}|^{q}\geq\sum_{(j,k)\in S_{i}(\eta)}|g_{i,j,k}^{*}|^{q}\geq\eta^{q}|S_{i}(\eta)|,

and

\|(\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}=\sum_{(j,k)\in S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}|g_{i,j,k}^{*}|=\sum_{(j,k)\in S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}|g_{i,j,k}^{*}|^{q}|g_{i,j,k}^{*}|^{1-q}.

Thus, we have

|S_{i}(\eta)|\leq R_{i,q}\eta^{-q}\quad\text{and}\quad\|(\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}\leq R_{i,q}\eta^{1-q}.

(S18)

Similarly, we can show that

|S_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}(\eta)|\leq R_{i,q}^{\mathrm{MA}}\eta^{-q}\quad\text{and}\quad\|(\boldsymbol{g}^{*}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}})_{S_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}\leq R_{i,q}^{\mathrm{MA}}\eta^{1-q}.

(S19)

By (
S19
), by choosing
\eta
such that

\eta^{2-q}\leq\frac{(r+2s)\overline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{2}}{R_{i,q}^{\mathrm{MA}}},

(S20)

we have

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}^{2}\leq 2\|(\boldsymbol{g}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*})_{S_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}(\eta)}\|_{1}^{2}+2\|(\boldsymbol{g}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*})_{S_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2|S_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}(\eta)|\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}+2(R_{i,q}^{\mathrm{MA}}\eta^{1-q})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2R_{i,q}^{\mathrm{MA}}\eta^{-q}\left\{(r+2s)\overline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{2}+R_{i,q}^{\mathrm{MA}}\eta^{2-q}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq 4R_{i,q}^{\mathrm{MA}}\eta^{-q}(r+2s)\overline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{2}.

Then, since
r+2s\lesssim 1
and
(\overline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}/\underline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}})^{2}\lesssim R_{i,q}/R_{i,q}^{\mathrm{MA}}
, we further have

\underline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{-2}\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}^{2}\lesssim R_{i,q}\eta^{-q}.

(S21)

Consider the right-hand side of (
S6.4
). By Lemma
S9
, if we choose
\lambda_{g}
such that

\frac{\lambda_{g}}{4}\geq C_{{\mathrm{dev}}}\sqrt{\frac{\kappa_{2}\lambda_{\max}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon})\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}}{T}},

(S22)

then we can show that

\displaystyle\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\varepsilon_{i,t},\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\rangle+\lambda_{g}(\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*}\|_{1}-\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{g}}_{i}\|_{1})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\frac{\lambda_{g}}{2}(\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{d}}_{i}\|_{1}+\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\phi}}_{i}\|_{2})+\lambda_{g}(\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*}\|_{1}-\|\boldsymbol{g}_{S_{i}(\eta)}^{*}+(\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{i})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}+\|(\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}+(\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{i})_{S_{i}(\eta)}\|_{1})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\frac{\lambda_{g}}{2}(\|(\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{i})_{S_{i}(\eta)}\|_{1}+\|(\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{i})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}+\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\phi}}_{i}\|_{2})+\lambda_{g}(2\|(\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}+\|(\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{i})_{S_{i}(\eta)}\|_{1}-\|(\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{i})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\frac{\lambda_{g}}{2}\left(4\|(\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}+3\|(\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{i})_{S_{i}(\eta)}\|_{1}-\|(\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{i})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}+\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\phi}}_{i}\|_{2}\right).

(S23)

In addition, since
T\gtrsim\kappa_{2}(p\vee 1)^{4}
, it follows from Lemmas
S11
and
S12
that

\displaystyle S_{2}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i})-S_{1}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\lambda_{g}}{4}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{d}}_{i}\|_{1}+\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\phi}}_{i}\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\lambda_{g}}{4}\left(\|(\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{i})_{S_{i}(\eta)}\|_{1}+\|(\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{i})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}+\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\phi}}_{i}\|_{2}\right).

(S24)

Combining (
S6.4
), (
S6.4
) and (
S6.4
), we have

0\leq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{x}}_{t})^{2}\leq\frac{\lambda_{g}}{4}\left(8\|(\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}+7\|(\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{i})_{S_{i}(\eta)}\|_{1}-\|(\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{i})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}+3\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\phi}}_{i}\|_{2}\right),

which implies

\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{d}}_{i}\|_{1}=\|(\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{i})_{S_{i}(\eta)}\|_{1}+\|(\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{i})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}\leq 8\|(\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}+8\|(\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{i})_{S_{i}(\eta)}\|_{1}+3\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\phi}}_{i}\|_{2}.

Then, by the Cauchy-Schwarz inequalty, (
S14
), (
S18
), and (
S21
), we can further show that

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{d}}_{i}\|_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3\left(64\|(\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}^{2}+64\|(\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{i})_{S_{i}(\eta)}\|_{1}^{2}+9\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\phi}}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 192\|(\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}^{2}+c_{\Delta}^{-2}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\left\{192|S_{i}(\eta)|+27\underline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{-2}\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq 192\|(\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}^{2}+C_{1}c_{\Delta}^{-2}R_{i,q}\eta^{-q}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}\|_{2}^{2},

(S25)

for an absolute constant
C_{1}>0
.
Similarly, from (
S6.4
) and (
S6.4
), we can deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\varepsilon_{i,t},\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\rangle+\lambda_{g}(\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*}\|_{1}-\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{g}}_{i}\|_{1})+S_{2}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i})-S_{1}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\frac{\lambda_{g}}{4}\left(8\|(\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}+8\|(\widehat{\boldsymbol{d}}_{i})_{S_{i}(\eta)}\|_{1}+3\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{*}\|_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\phi}}_{i}\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\frac{\lambda_{g}}{2}\left\{4\|(\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}+C_{2}c_{\Delta}^{-1}R_{i,q}^{1/2}\eta^{-q/2}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}\|_{2}\right\},

(S26)

for an absolute constant
C_{2}>0
.

By Lemmas
S10
and
S13
, we can show that

\frac{3}{4T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{x}_{t})^{2}-S_{3}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i})\geq\frac{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}\kappa_{1}}{2}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}-\frac{\kappa_{2}}{T}\left\{C_{{\mathrm{init}}3}(p\vee 1)+\frac{3}{4}C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}\frac{\kappa_{2}}{\kappa_{1}}\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}\right\}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{d}}_{i}\|_{1}^{2}.

which, in conjunction with (
S6.4
), leads to

\frac{3}{4T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{x}_{t})^{2}-S_{3}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i})\geq\frac{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}\kappa_{1}}{4}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}-\frac{C_{3}\kappa_{2}^{2}(p\vee 1)\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}}{\kappa_{1}T}\|(\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}^{2},

(S27)

where
C_{3}>0
is an absolute constant, if we further have

T\gtrsim R_{i,q}\eta^{-q}(\kappa_{2}/\kappa_{1})^{2}(p\vee 1)\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}.

(S28)

Combining (
S17
), (
S6.4
), and (
S27
), we have

\frac{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}\kappa_{1}}{4}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}-\frac{C_{3}\kappa_{2}^{2}(p\vee 1)\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}}{\kappa_{1}T}\|(\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}^{2}\leq\frac{\lambda_{g}}{2}\left\{4\|(\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}+C_{2}c_{\Delta}^{-1}R_{i,q}^{1/2}\eta^{-q/2}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}\|_{2}\right\}.

Consider the following two cases.

Case (i):
First suppose that

\frac{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}\kappa_{1}}{8}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\geq\frac{C_{3}\kappa_{2}^{2}(p\vee 1)\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}}{\kappa_{1}T}\|(\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}^{2}
.
Then

\frac{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}\kappa_{1}}{8}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{\lambda_{g}}{2}\left\{4\|(\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}+C_{2}c_{\Delta}^{-1}R_{i,q}^{1/2}\eta^{-q/2}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}\|_{2}\right\},

which involves a quadratic form in
\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}\|_{2}
. By computing the zeros of this quadratic form, we can show that

\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{32C_{2}^{2}}{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}^{2}c_{\Delta}^{2}}\cdot\frac{\lambda_{g}^{2}R_{i,q}\eta^{-q}}{\kappa_{1}^{2}}+\frac{32}{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}}\cdot\frac{\lambda_{g}\|(\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}}{\kappa_{1}}.

Case (ii):
Otherwise, we must have
\frac{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}\kappa_{1}}{8}\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{C_{3}\kappa_{2}^{2}(p\vee 1)\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}}{\kappa_{1}T}\|(\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}^{2}
.

Combining the two cases above, we can apply (
S18
) and (
S28
) to show that

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{32C_{2}^{2}}{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}^{2}c_{\Delta}^{2}}\cdot\frac{\lambda_{g}^{2}R_{i,q}\eta^{-q}}{\kappa_{1}^{2}}+\frac{32}{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}}\cdot\frac{\lambda_{g}\|(\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}}{\kappa_{1}}+\frac{8C_{3}}{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}}\cdot\frac{\kappa_{2}^{2}(p\vee 1)\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}}{\kappa_{1}^{2}T}\|(\boldsymbol{g}_{i}^{*})_{S_{i}^{\complement}(\eta)}\|_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{32C_{2}^{2}}{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}^{2}c_{\Delta}^{2}}\cdot\frac{\lambda_{g}^{2}R_{i,q}\eta^{-q}}{\kappa_{1}^{2}}+\frac{32}{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}}\cdot\frac{\lambda_{g}R_{i,q}\eta^{1-q}}{\kappa_{1}}+\frac{8C_{3}}{C_{{\mathrm{rsc}}}}\cdot(R_{i,q}\eta^{-q})^{-1}(R_{i,q}\eta^{1-q})^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\frac{\lambda_{g}}{\kappa_{1}}\right)^{2-q}R_{i,q}=\eta^{2-q}R_{i,q},

if we choose

\eta=\frac{\lambda_{g}}{\kappa_{1}}.

Thus, taking
\lambda_{g}
as its lower bound in (
S22
), i.e.,

\lambda_{g}\asymp\sqrt{\kappa_{2}\lambda_{\max}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon})\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}/T}
, we have

\|\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\lesssim\left[\frac{\kappa_{2}\lambda_{\max}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon})\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}}{\kappa_{1}^{2}T}\right]^{1-q/2}R_{i,q},

and subsequently,

\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{x}}_{t})^{2}\lesssim\lambda_{g}\eta^{1-q}R_{i,q}=\left[\frac{\kappa_{2}\lambda_{\max}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon})\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}}{\kappa_{1}^{2}T}\right]^{1-q/2}\frac{R_{i,q}}{\kappa_{1}^{1-q}},

where the latter follows from (
S6.4
) and (
S6.4
).
On the one hand, with the above choice of
\eta
, condition (
S28
) can be guaranteed if

R_{i,q}\lesssim\frac{\lambda_{\max}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon})}{\kappa_{2}(p\vee 1)}\cdot\left[\frac{\kappa_{1}^{2}T}{\kappa_{2}\lambda_{\max}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon})\log\{N(p\vee 1)\}}\right]^{1-q/2}.

(S29)

Under condition (
S29
), since
r+2s\lesssim 1
, we can show that a sufficient condition for (
S20
) is

\frac{\lambda_{\max}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon})}{\kappa_{2}(p\vee 1)}\lesssim\overline{\alpha}_{i,{\mathrm{MA}}}^{2}R_{i,q}/R_{i,q}^{\mathrm{MA}}.

(S30)

Finally, combining the tail probabilities in Lemmas
S9
–
S13
and the required conditions including (
S29
) and (
S30
), we accomplish the proof of this theorem.

S7
Proof of Theorem
4

S7.1
Irreducibility condition

Lemma
S14
provides the irreducibility condition for the orders
(p,r,s)
of model (
2.4
). To better understand result (i) in this lemma, it is worth noting that the order
p
has a more intricate impact on the parameterization than
r
and
s
, due to the dependence of the functions
\ell_{h,k}(\cdot)
’s on
p
. For example, suppose that
(p,r,s)=(1,1,0)
, i.e.,
\boldsymbol{y}_{t}=\boldsymbol{G}_{1}\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}+\sum_{h=2}^{\infty}\lambda_{1}^{h-1}\boldsymbol{G}_{2}\boldsymbol{y}_{t-h}+\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{t}
. Decreasing
p
to zero leads to the reduced model

\boldsymbol{y}_{t}=\sum_{h=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{1}^{h}\boldsymbol{G}\boldsymbol{y}_{t-h}+\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{t}
.
Note that the latter cannot be obtained by simply setting
\boldsymbol{G}_{1}=\boldsymbol{0}
. However, if the equality
\boldsymbol{G}_{1}=\boldsymbol{G}_{2}
is satisfied, then the reduced model will be fulfilled with
\boldsymbol{G}=\lambda_{1}^{-1}\boldsymbol{G}_{1}
.

Lemma S14

(Irreducibility of model orders)
.

Consider the parameterization of
\boldsymbol{A}_{h}
for
h\geq 1
with model orders
(p,r,s)
in (
2.3
), i.e.,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\boldsymbol{A}_{h}&=\sum_{k=1}^{p}\mathbb{I}_{\{h=k\}}\boldsymbol{G}_{k}+\sum_{j=1}^{r}\mathbb{I}_{\{h\geq p+1\}}\lambda_{j}^{h-p}\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\mathbb{I}_{\{h\geq p+1\}}\gamma_{m}^{h-p}\left[\cos\{(h-p)\theta_{m}\}\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m-1}+\sin\{(h-p)\theta_{m}\}\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m}\right],\end{split}

(S1)

where
\lambda_{j}\in(-1,1)
for
1\leq j\leq r
are distinct, and
\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}=(\gamma_{m},\theta_{m})^{\top}\in\boldsymbol{\varPi}
for
1\leq m\leq s
are distinct, with
\boldsymbol{\varPi}=[0,1)\times(0,\pi)
.

(i)

If
\boldsymbol{G}_{p}=\sum_{j=1}^{r}\mathbb{I}_{\{\lambda_{j}\neq 0\}}\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\mathbb{I}_{\{\gamma_{m}\neq 0\}}\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m-1}
, then the order
p
can be reduced to
p-1
. Otherwise, the order
p
is irreducible.

(ii)

If there exists
1\leq j\leq r
such that
\lambda_{j}=0
or
\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}=\boldsymbol{0}
, then the order
r
can be reduced to
r-1
. Otherwise, the order
r
is irreducible.

(iii)

If there exists
1\leq m\leq s
such that
\gamma_{m}=0
or
\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m-1}=\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m}=\boldsymbol{0}
, then the order
s
can be reduced to
s-1
. Otherwise, the order
s
is irreducible.

Proof of Lemma
S14
.

Let us first prove (i). Let
\widetilde{p}=p-1
. If
\boldsymbol{G}_{p}=\sum_{j=1}^{r}\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m-1}
, then it can be readily verified that for
h\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\boldsymbol{A}_{h}&=\sum_{k=1}^{\widetilde{p}}\mathbb{I}_{\{h=k\}}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{G}}_{k}+\sum_{j=1}^{r}\mathbb{I}_{\{h\geq\widetilde{p}+1\}}\lambda_{j}^{h-\widetilde{p}}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{G}}_{\widetilde{p}+j}\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\mathbb{I}_{\{h\geq\widetilde{p}+1\}}\gamma_{m}^{h-\widetilde{p}}\left[\cos\{(h-\widetilde{p})\theta_{m}\}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{G}}_{\widetilde{p}+r+2m-1}+\sin\{(h-\widetilde{p})\theta_{m}\}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{G}}_{\widetilde{p}+r+2m}\right],\end{split}

(S2)

where
\boldsymbol{\widetilde{G}}_{k}=\boldsymbol{G}_{k}
for
1\leq k\leq\widetilde{p}
,
\boldsymbol{\widetilde{G}}_{\widetilde{p}+j}=\mathbb{I}_{\{\lambda_{j}\neq 0\}}\lambda_{j}^{-1}\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}
for
1\leq j\leq r
,
and

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\widetilde{G}}_{\widetilde{p}+r+2m-1}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{I}_{\{\gamma_{m}\neq 0\}}\gamma_{m}^{-1}\left\{\cos(\theta_{m})\boldsymbol{G}_{\widetilde{p}+r+2m-1}-\sin(\theta_{m})\boldsymbol{G}_{\widetilde{p}+r+2m}\right\},

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\widetilde{G}}_{\widetilde{p}+r+2m}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{I}_{\{\gamma_{m}\neq 0\}}\gamma_{m}^{-1}\left\{\sin(\theta_{m})\boldsymbol{G}_{\widetilde{p}+r+2m-1}+\cos(\theta_{m})\boldsymbol{G}_{\widetilde{p}+r+2m}\right\},

for
1\leq m\leq s
. In other words, the order
p
can be reduced to
\widetilde{p}
.

Now suppose that
\boldsymbol{G}_{p}\neq\sum_{j=1}^{r}\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m-1}
. If (
S1
) can be reduced to the form in (
S2
), then we must have
\boldsymbol{G}_{k}=\boldsymbol{\widetilde{G}}_{k}
for
1\leq k\leq\widetilde{p}
,

\boldsymbol{G}_{p}=\sum_{j=1}^{r}\lambda_{j}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{G}}_{\widetilde{p}+j}\\
+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\left\{(\gamma_{m}\cos\theta_{m})\boldsymbol{\widetilde{G}}_{\widetilde{p}+r+2m-1}+(\gamma_{m}\sin\theta_{m})\boldsymbol{\widetilde{G}}_{\widetilde{p}+r+2m}\right\},

\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}=\lambda_{j}\boldsymbol{\widetilde{G}}_{\widetilde{p}+j}
for
1\leq j\leq r
, and

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{G}_{\widetilde{p}+r+2m-1}

\displaystyle=\gamma_{m}\cos(\theta_{m})\boldsymbol{\widetilde{G}}_{\widetilde{p}+r+2m-1}+\gamma_{m}\sin(\theta_{m})\boldsymbol{\widetilde{G}}_{\widetilde{p}+r+2m},

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{G}_{\widetilde{p}+r+2m}

\displaystyle=-\gamma_{m}\sin(\theta_{m})\boldsymbol{\widetilde{G}}_{\widetilde{p}+r+2m-1}+\gamma_{m}\cos(\theta_{m})\boldsymbol{\widetilde{G}}_{\widetilde{p}+r+2m},

for
1\leq m\leq s
. However, this implies
\boldsymbol{G}_{p}=\sum_{j=1}^{r}\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m-1}
, resulting in a contradiction. Thus, (i) is proved.

To establish (ii) and (iii), it is helpful to rewrite (
S1
) in the form of

\displaystyle\begin{split}\boldsymbol{A}_{h}&=\sum_{k=1}^{p}\mathbb{I}_{\{h=k\}}\boldsymbol{G}_{k}+\sum_{j=1}^{r}\mathbb{I}_{\{h\geq p+1\}}\lambda_{j}^{h-p}\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\mathbb{I}_{\{h\geq p+1\}}\left\{v_{m}^{h-p}\boldsymbol{H}_{p+r+2m-1}+u_{m}^{h-p}\boldsymbol{H}_{p+r+2m}\right\},\quad h\geq 1,\end{split}

(S3)

where
v_{m}=\gamma_{m}e^{i\theta_{m}}
,
u_{m}=\gamma_{m}e^{-i\theta_{m}}
,

\boldsymbol{H}_{p+r+2m-1}=(\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m-1}-i\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m})/2
,
and

\boldsymbol{H}_{p+r+2m}=(\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m-1}+i\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m})/2
,
for
1\leq m\leq s
, with
i
denoting the imaginary unit. Note that
\boldsymbol{H}_{p+r+2m-1}=\boldsymbol{H}_{p+r+2m}=\boldsymbol{0}
if and only if
\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m-1}=\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m}=\boldsymbol{0}
. Then the first part of (ii) and (iii) is obvious.

Lastly, note that if
\gamma_{m}\neq 0
for
1\leq m\leq s
, then
v_{1},\dots,v_{s},u_{1},\dots,u_{s}
are all distinct and nonzero. As a result, the second part of (ii) and (iii) is a straightforward consequence of the linear independence of exponential functions.
∎

S7.2
Reparameterization with maximum orders

We show that any model of order
\pazocal{M}=(p,r,s)\in\mathscr{M}=\{(p,r,s)\mid 0\leq p\leq\overline{p},0\leq r\leq\overline{r},0\leq s\leq\overline{s}\}
can be expressed as one of maximum orders
\overline{\pazocal{M}}=(\overline{p},\overline{r},\overline{s})
, with the corresponding parameters determined by the original ones.
Let
\delta_{p}=\overline{p}-p
,
\delta_{r}=\overline{r}-r
,
\delta_{s}=\overline{s}-s
, and
\delta_{d}=\overline{d}-d
. The proof of Lemma
S15
is straightforward by elementary algebra.

Lemma S15

(Reparameterization with maximum orders)
.

Suppose that
\boldsymbol{A}_{h}=\boldsymbol{A}_{h}(\boldsymbol{\omega},\boldsymbol{g})
for
h\geq 1
is parameterized as in (
S1
) with model orders
\pazocal{M}=(p,r,s)\in\mathscr{M}
, where
\boldsymbol{{\omega}}\in(-1,1)^{r}\times\boldsymbol{\varPi}^{s}
and
\boldsymbol{{g}}\in\mathbb{R}^{N^{2}d}
.
Then
\boldsymbol{A}_{h}
for
h\geq 1
can be expressed with orders
\overline{\pazocal{M}}=(\overline{p},\overline{r},\overline{s})
as follows,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\boldsymbol{A}_{h}(\boldsymbol{\overline{\omega}},\boldsymbol{\overline{g}})&=\sum_{k=1}^{\overline{p}}\mathbb{I}_{\{h=k\}}\overline{\boldsymbol{G}}_{k}+\sum_{j=1}^{\overline{r}}\mathbb{I}_{\{h\geq\overline{p}+1\}}\overline{\lambda}_{j}^{h-\overline{p}}\overline{\boldsymbol{G}}_{\overline{p}+j}\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt+\sum_{m=1}^{\overline{s}}\mathbb{I}_{\{h\geq\overline{p}+1\}}\overline{\gamma}_{m}^{h-\overline{p}}\left[\cos\{(h-\overline{p})\overline{\theta}_{m}\}\overline{\boldsymbol{G}}_{\overline{p}+\overline{r}+2m-1}+\sin\{(h-\overline{p})\overline{\theta}_{m}\}\overline{\boldsymbol{G}}_{\overline{p}+\overline{r}+2m}\right],\end{split}

where the parameter vector
\boldsymbol{\overline{\omega}}=(\overline{\lambda}_{1},\dots,\overline{\lambda}_{\overline{r}},\boldsymbol{\overline{\eta}}_{1}^{\top},\dots,\boldsymbol{\overline{\eta}}_{\overline{s}}^{\top})^{\top}\in(-1,1)^{\overline{r}}\times\boldsymbol{\varPi}^{\overline{s}}
and the matrices
\overline{\boldsymbol{G}}_{k}
for
1\leq k\leq\overline{d}
are given by

\displaystyle\overline{\lambda}_{j}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{I}_{\{1\leq j\leq r\}}\lambda_{j}\quad\text{for}\quad 1\leq j\leq\overline{r},\quad\boldsymbol{\overline{\eta}}_{m}=\mathbb{I}_{\{1\leq m\leq s\}}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{m}\quad\text{for}\quad 1\leq m\leq\overline{s},

\displaystyle\overline{\boldsymbol{G}}_{k}

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{G}_{k}\quad\text{for}\quad 1\leq k\leq p,

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\overline{G}}_{p+k}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{r}\lambda_{j}^{k}\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\sum_{m=1}^{s}\gamma_{m}^{k}\left\{\cos(k\theta_{m})\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m-1}+\sin(k\theta_{m})\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m}\right\}\quad\text{for}\quad 1\leq k\leq\delta_{p},

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\overline{G}}_{\overline{p}+j}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{I}_{\{1\leq j\leq r\}}\lambda_{j}^{\delta_{p}}\boldsymbol{G}_{p+j}\quad\text{for}\quad 1\leq j\leq\overline{r},

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\overline{G}}_{\overline{p}+\overline{r}+2m-1}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{I}_{\{1\leq m\leq s\}}\gamma_{m}^{\delta_{p}}\left\{\cos(\delta_{p}\theta_{m})\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m-1}+\sin(\delta_{p}\theta_{m})\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m}\right\}\quad\text{for}\quad 1\leq m\leq\overline{s},

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\overline{G}}_{\overline{p}+\overline{r}+2m}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{I}_{\{1\leq m\leq s\}}\gamma_{m}^{\delta_{p}}\left\{-\sin(\delta_{p}\theta_{m})\boldsymbol{g}_{i,p+r+2m}+\cos(\delta_{p}\theta_{m})\boldsymbol{G}_{p+r+2m}\right\}\quad\text{for}\quad 1\leq m\leq\overline{s},

and
\boldsymbol{\overline{g}}=\vect(\boldsymbol{\overline{G}})
with
\boldsymbol{\overline{G}}=(\boldsymbol{\overline{G}}_{1},\dots,\boldsymbol{\overline{G}}_{\overline{d}})\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times N\overline{d}}
.

S7.3
Restricted parameter space

Based on Lemma
S15
, this section provides a useful intermediate result for the proof of Theorem
4
. It allows us to establish a connection between the parameter space of any
\pazocal{M}\in\mathscr{M}_{\textup{mis}}
and that of
\pazocal{M}^{*}
; see Proposition
S4
below.

The relationship between
(\boldsymbol{\overline{\omega}},\boldsymbol{\overline{g}})
and
(\boldsymbol{\omega},\boldsymbol{g})
in Lemma
S15
can be equivalently written as

\overline{\boldsymbol{\omega}}=\boldsymbol{\overline{R}}_{1}^{\pazocal{M}}\boldsymbol{\omega}\quad\text{and}\quad\overline{\boldsymbol{g}}=(\boldsymbol{\overline{R}}_{2}^{\pazocal{M}}(\boldsymbol{\omega})\otimes\boldsymbol{I}_{N^{2}})\boldsymbol{g}.

(S4)

Here
\boldsymbol{\overline{R}}_{1}^{\pazocal{M}}
is a
(\overline{r}+2\overline{s})\times(r+2s)
constant matrix,

\boldsymbol{\overline{R}}_{1}^{\pazocal{M}}=\left(\begin{array}[]{ll}\boldsymbol{I}_{r}&\boldsymbol{0}_{r\times 2s}\\
\boldsymbol{0}_{\delta_{r}\times r}&\boldsymbol{0}_{\delta_{r}\times 2s}\\
\boldsymbol{0}&\boldsymbol{I}_{2s}\\
\boldsymbol{0}&\boldsymbol{0}_{2\delta_{s}\times 2s}\end{array}\right),

and the function
\boldsymbol{\overline{R}}_{2}^{\pazocal{M}}:(-1,1)^{r}\times\boldsymbol{\varPi}^{s}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{\overline{d}\times d}
is defined as

\boldsymbol{\overline{R}}_{2}^{\pazocal{M}}(\boldsymbol{\omega})=\left(\begin{array}[]{lll}\boldsymbol{I}_{p}&\boldsymbol{0}_{p\times r}&\boldsymbol{0}_{p\times 2s}\\
\boldsymbol{0}_{\delta_{p}\times p}&\boldsymbol{L}_{1}(\boldsymbol{\lambda})&\boldsymbol{L}_{2}(\boldsymbol{\eta})\\
\boldsymbol{0}_{r\times p}&\boldsymbol{D}_{1}(\boldsymbol{\lambda})&\boldsymbol{0}_{r\times 2s}\\
\boldsymbol{0}_{\delta_{r}\times p}&\boldsymbol{0}_{\delta_{r}\times r}&\boldsymbol{0}_{\delta_{r}\times 2s}\\
\boldsymbol{0}&\boldsymbol{0}_{2s\times r}&\boldsymbol{D}_{2}(\boldsymbol{\eta})\\
\boldsymbol{0}&\boldsymbol{0}_{2\delta_{s}\times r}&\boldsymbol{0}_{2\delta_{s}\times 2s}\end{array}\right),

where
\boldsymbol{L}_{1}(\boldsymbol{\lambda})
is a
\delta_{p}\times r
matrix whose
k
th row is
(\lambda_{1}^{k},\dots,\lambda_{r}^{k})
,
\boldsymbol{L}_{2}(\boldsymbol{\eta})
is a
\delta_{p}\times 2s
matrix whose
k
th row is
